# Test coverage Linear proposal, 2026-11-25 (phase 1, nothing filed)

First run: no issue carries the label "Test Coverage" in any team, so every repository and type pair in the gap list gets one new issue (450 types; the request said 449, the list holds 450).

## Label choices

- Workspace label "Test Coverage": create (does not exist), on every issue.
- "1 - Type": **Add** (the issue adds tests; the existing test backfill issues such as KTRL5-7 and KTRUI-10 use Add). "Verify" is described as "no code change expected", so it does not fit.
- "4 - Function": **Testing and documentation** (exists, shared by every team).
- Estimate by untested members: 1 for 1 to 2, 2 for 3 to 5, 4 for 6 to 10, 8 for more; a type with only a "type not named" row: 2; a type with the type row plus member rows: by member rows. Assignee "me", no priority, state Backlog.
- Duplicates: none (list_issues "Tests:" in all 8 teams, archived included: no issue titled "Tests: <Type> ...").
